==============================================================================
### 2.c ChordCalculator.kt (DetectedChord + detect())
### FICHIER : app/src/main/java/com/soundstage/mixer/ui/components/ChordCalculator.kt
==============================================================================

package com.soundstage.mixer.ui.components

/**
 * Professional Ultra-Fast Real-Time Chord Calculator & Music Theory Engine.
 * Analyzes active polyphonic notes with zero latency and calculates:
 * - Extended Chords (9th, 11th, 13th, altered dominants, sus, add9, 6/9, slash inversions).
 * - Jazz open voicings with omitted 5th (no5) or roots.
 * - Comprehensive chord dictionary with rich alternate naming (e.g., Δ, -, ø, dim, alt, Hendrix).
 * - Real-time interval breakdown and notes list.
 */
data class DetectedChord(
    val primaryName: String,
    val equivalentName: String,
    val variantName: String = "",
    val alternateNames: String = "",
    val alternateName2: String = "",
    val formula: String,
    val notesList: List<String>
)

object ChordCalculator {

    private val NOTE_NAMES = listOf("C", "C#", "D", "D#", "E", "F", "F#", "G", "G#", "A", "A#", "B")

    // Map of sorted interval sets (modulo 12 relative to root = 0) -> (Suffix, Triple(Variant Name Suffix/Format, Alternate Notation, Interval Formula))
    private val CHORD_DEFINITIONS: Map<List<Int>, Triple<String, String, String>> = mapOf(
        // --- 1. Basic Triads ---
        listOf(0, 4, 7) to Triple("", "Maj", "1 - 3 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 3, 7) to Triple("m", "Min", "1 - b3 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 3, 6) to Triple("dim", "°", "1 - b3 - b5"),
        listOf(0, 4, 8) to Triple("aug", "+", "1 - 3 - #5"),
        listOf(0, 5, 7) to Triple("sus4", "sus", "1 - 4 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 2, 7) to Triple("sus2", "sus2", "1 - 2 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 2, 5, 7) to Triple("sus2sus4", "sus24", "1 - 2 - 4 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 7) to Triple("add9", "add2", "1 - 2 - 3 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 2, 3, 7) to Triple("m(add9)", "m(add2)", "1 - 2 - b3 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 4, 5, 7) to Triple("add11", "add4", "1 - 3 - 4 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 3, 5, 7) to Triple("m(add11)", "m(add4)", "1 - b3 - 4 - 5"),

        // --- 2. 7th Chords ---
        listOf(0, 4, 7, 10) to Triple("7", "dom7", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 4, 7, 11) to Triple("maj7", "Δ7", "1 - 3 - 5 - 7"),
        listOf(0, 3, 7, 10) to Triple("m7", "-7", "1 - b3 - 5 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 3, 7, 11) to Triple("m(maj7)", "-Δ7", "1 - b3 - 5 - 7"),
        listOf(0, 3, 6, 10) to Triple("m7b5", "ø7", "1 - b3 - b5 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 3, 6, 9) to Triple("dim7", "°7", "1 - b3 - b5 - bb7"),
        listOf(0, 4, 8, 10) to Triple("7#5", "7+", "1 - 3 - #5 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 4, 6, 10) to Triple("7b5", "7(b5)", "1 - 3 - b5 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 4, 8, 11) to Triple("maj7#5", "Δ#5", "1 - 3 - #5 - 7"),
        listOf(0, 4, 6, 11) to Triple("maj7b5", "Δb5", "1 - 3 - b5 - 7"),
        listOf(0, 5, 7, 10) to Triple("7sus4", "7sus", "1 - 4 - 5 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 2, 7, 10) to Triple("7sus2", "sus2(7)", "1 - 2 - 5 - b7"),

        // --- 3. 6th & 6/9 Chords ---
        listOf(0, 4, 7, 9) to Triple("6", "maj6", "1 - 3 - 5 - 6"),
        listOf(0, 3, 7, 9) to Triple("m6", "-6", "1 - b3 - 5 - 6"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 7, 9) to Triple("6/9", "6(add9)", "1 - 3 - 5 - 6 - 9"),
        listOf(0, 2, 3, 7, 9) to Triple("m6/9", "-6(add9)", "1 - b3 - 5 - 6 - 9"),

        // --- 4. 9th Chords ---
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 7, 10) to Triple("9", "dom9", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - 9"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 7, 11) to Triple("maj9", "Δ9", "1 - 3 - 5 - 7 - 9"),
        listOf(0, 2, 3, 7, 10) to Triple("m9", "-9", "1 - b3 - 5 - b7 - 9"),
        listOf(0, 2, 3, 7, 11) to Triple("m(maj9)", "-Δ9", "1 - b3 - 5 - 7 - 9"),
        listOf(0, 1, 4, 7, 10) to Triple("7b9", "7(b9)", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - b9"),
        listOf(0, 3, 4, 7, 10) to Triple("7#9", "7(#9)", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - #9"),
        listOf(0, 2, 3, 6, 10) to Triple("m9b5", "ø9", "1 - b3 - b5 - b7 - 9"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 8, 10) to Triple("9#5", "9(+5)", "1 - 3 - #5 - b7 - 9"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 6, 10) to Triple("9b5", "9(b5)", "1 - 3 - b5 - b7 - 9"),
        listOf(0, 2, 5, 7, 10) to Triple("9sus4", "9sus", "1 - 4 - 5 - b7 - 9"),

        // --- 5. 11th Extended Chords ---
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 5, 7, 10) to Triple("11", "dom11", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - 9 - 11"),
        listOf(0, 2, 3, 5, 7, 10) to Triple("m11", "-11", "1 - b3 - 5 - b7 - 9 - 11"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 5, 7, 11) to Triple("maj11", "Δ11", "1 - 3 - 5 - 7 - 9 - 11"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 6, 7, 10) to Triple("7#11", "7(+11)", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - 9 - #11"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 6, 7, 11) to Triple("maj7#11", "Δ#11", "1 - 3 - 5 - 7 - 9 - #11"),
        listOf(0, 2, 3, 5, 6, 10) to Triple("m11b5", "ø11", "1 - b3 - b5 - b7 - 9 - 11"),
        listOf(0, 1, 4, 5, 7, 10) to Triple("11b9", "11(b9)", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - b9 - 11"),

        // --- 6. 13th Extended Chords ---
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 7, 9, 10) to Triple("13", "dom13", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - 9 - 13"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 7, 9, 11) to Triple("maj13", "Δ13", "1 - 3 - 5 - 7 - 9 - 13"),
        listOf(0, 2, 3, 7, 9, 10) to Triple("m13", "-13", "1 - b3 - 5 - b7 - 9 - 13"),
        listOf(0, 1, 4, 7, 9, 10) to Triple("13b9", "13(b9)", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - b9 - 13"),
        listOf(0, 3, 4, 7, 9, 10) to Triple("13#9", "13(#9)", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - #9 - 13"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 6, 9, 10) to Triple("13#11", "13(#11)", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - 9 - #11 - 13"),
        listOf(0, 2, 4, 8, 9, 10) to Triple("7b13", "7(b13)", "1 - 3 - 5 - b7 - b13"),
        listOf(0, 2, 5, 7, 9, 10) to Triple("13sus4", "13sus", "1 - 4 - 5 - b7 - 9 - 13"),

        // --- 7. Altered Chords (Jazz Super Locrian) ---
        listOf(0, 1, 4, 8, 10) to Triple("7alt", "7(b9,b13)", "1 - 3 - #5 - b7 - b9"),
        listOf(0, 3, 4, 8, 10) to Triple("7alt(#9)", "7(#9,b13)", "1 - 3 - #5 - b7 - #9"),
        listOf(0, 1, 4, 6, 10) to Triple("7b9b5", "7(b9,b5)", "1 - 3 - b5 - b7 - b9"),
        listOf(0, 3, 4, 6, 10) to Triple("7#9b5", "7(#9,b5)", "1 - 3 - b5 - b7 - #9"),

        // --- 8. Jazz Voicings with Omitted 5th (no5) ---
        listOf(0, 4, 10) to Triple("7(no5)", "dom7", "1 - 3 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 4, 11) to Triple("maj7(no5)", "Δ7", "1 - 3 - 7"),
        listOf(0, 3, 10) to Triple("m7(no5)", "-7", "1 - b3 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 3, 11) to Triple("m(maj7)(no5)", "-Δ7", "1 - b3 - 7"),
        listOf(0, 2, 10) to Triple("7sus2(no5)", "7sus2", "1 - 2 - b7"),
        listOf(0, 5, 10) to Triple("7sus4(no5)", "7sus", "1 - 4 - b7"),

        // --- 9. Two-note Dyads / Power Chords ---
        listOf(0, 7) to Triple("5", "P5", "1 - 5"),
        listOf(0, 4) to Triple("(no5)", "3rd", "1 - 3"),
        listOf(0, 3) to Triple("m(no5)", "m3rd", "1 - b3"),
        listOf(0, 5) to Triple("sus4(no5)", "4th", "1 - 4"),
        listOf(0, 2) to Triple("sus2(no5)", "2nd", "1 - 2")
    )

    fun parsePitchClass(noteStr: String): Int? {
        val clean = noteStr.trim().uppercase()
        if (clean.isEmpty()) return null
        val letter = when {
            clean.startsWith("C#") || clean.startsWith("DB") -> "C#"
            clean.startsWith("D#") || clean.startsWith("EB") -> "D#"
            clean.startsWith("F#") || clean.startsWith("GB") -> "F#"
            clean.startsWith("G#") || clean.startsWith("AB") -> "G#"
            clean.startsWith("A#") || clean.startsWith("BB") -> "A#"
            clean.startsWith("C") -> "C"
            clean.startsWith("D") -> "D"
            clean.startsWith("E") -> "E"
            clean.startsWith("F") -> "F"
            clean.startsWith("G") -> "G"
            clean.startsWith("A") -> "A"
            clean.startsWith("B") -> "B"
            else -> return null
        }
        val idx = NOTE_NAMES.indexOf(letter)
        return if (idx >= 0) idx else null
    }

    /**
     * Detects chord from polyphonic collection of notes.
     * Computes BOTH:
     * - Primary Name (e.g. Slash / Bass representation or direct name like F/D)
     * - Equivalent Name (e.g. Functional root chord like Dm7)
     */
    fun detect(notes: Collection<String>): DetectedChord? {
        if (notes.isEmpty()) return null

        val pitchClassesWithOctave = notes.mapNotNull { noteStr ->
            val pc = parsePitchClass(noteStr) ?: return@mapNotNull null
            val octave = noteStr.filter { it.isDigit() }.toIntOrNull() ?: 4
            val midi = octave * 12 + pc
            Triple(pc, midi, noteStr)
        }.sortedBy { it.second }

        if (pitchClassesWithOctave.isEmpty()) return null

        val uniquePitchClasses = pitchClassesWithOctave.map { it.first }.distinct()
        val lowestPitchClass = pitchClassesWithOctave.first().first
        val lowestNoteName = NOTE_NAMES[lowestPitchClass]

        // 1 Single Note
        if (uniquePitchClasses.size == 1) {
            val rootName = NOTE_NAMES[uniquePitchClasses.first()]
            return DetectedChord(
                primaryName = rootName,
                equivalentName = "",
                variantName = "",
                formula = "1",
                notesList = listOf(rootName)
            )
        }

        // Test potential chord roots and collect candidate interpretations
        val candidateChords = mutableListOf<Triple<Int, String, String>>() // (rootPc, chordName, formula)

        for (rootPc in uniquePitchClasses) {
            val rootName = NOTE_NAMES[rootPc]
            val intervals = uniquePitchClasses.map { (it - rootPc + 12) % 12 }.sorted()

            CHORD_DEFINITIONS[intervals]?.let { (suffix, _, formula) ->
                candidateChords.add(Triple(rootPc, "$rootName$suffix", formula))
            }
        }

        val notesFormatted = uniquePitchClasses.map { NOTE_NAMES[it] }.joinToString(" · ")

        if (candidateChords.isNotEmpty()) {
            // Find root-based chord matching the lowest bass note if possible
            val bassCandidate = candidateChords.firstOrNull { it.first == lowestPitchClass }
            // Find inverted candidate (where root != lowest bass)
            val nonBassCandidate = candidateChords.firstOrNull { it.first != lowestPitchClass }

            val primaryName: String
            val equivalentName: String
            val variantName: String

            if (nonBassCandidate != null && bassCandidate != null && nonBassCandidate.first != bassCandidate.first) {
                // Inverted or polychord voicing: e.g. bass D with F major triad -> Dm7 vs F/D
                primaryName = bassCandidate.second
                equivalentName = "${nonBassCandidate.second}/$lowestNoteName"
                variantName = nonBassCandidate.second
            } else if (bassCandidate != null) {
                primaryName = bassCandidate.second
                // Genuine music-theory pro alternate notation / substitution
                equivalentName = when {
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("maj7") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("maj7", "Δ7")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m7") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("m7", "-7")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("7") && !bassCandidate.second.endsWith("maj7") && !bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m7") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("7", "dom7")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m7b5") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("m7b5", "ø7")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("dim7") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("dim7", "°7")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("dim") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("dim", "°")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("aug") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("aug", "+")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("maj9") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("maj9", "Δ9")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m9") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("m9", "-9")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("9") && !bassCandidate.second.endsWith("maj9") && !bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m9") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("9", "dom9")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("maj11") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("maj11", "Δ11")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m11") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("m11", "-11")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("maj13") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("maj13", "Δ13")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m13") -> bassCandidate.second.replace("m13", "-13")
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("sus4") -> "${NOTE_NAMES[(lowestPitchClass + 5) % 12]}sus2/$lowestNoteName"
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("sus2") -> "${NOTE_NAMES[(lowestPitchClass + 7) % 12]}sus4/$lowestNoteName"
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("6") -> "${NOTE_NAMES[(lowestPitchClass + 9) % 12]}m7/$lowestNoteName"
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m6") -> "${NOTE_NAMES[(lowestPitchClass + 9) % 12]}m7b5/$lowestNoteName"
                    bassCandidate.second.endsWith("m") -> "${bassCandidate.second} Min"
                    bassCandidate.second == lowestNoteName -> "$lowestNoteName Maj"
                    else -> ""
                }
                variantName = if (equivalentName.isNotEmpty()) equivalentName else bassCandidate.third
            } else {
                // Inverted slash chord (lowest bass is an inversion note)
                val topCandidate = candidateChords.first()
                primaryName = "${topCandidate.second}/$lowestNoteName"
                equivalentName = topCandidate.second
                variantName = topCandidate.second
            }

            return DetectedChord(
                primaryName = primaryName,
                equivalentName = equivalentName,
                variantName = variantName,
                formula = notesFormatted,
                notesList = uniquePitchClasses.map { NOTE_NAMES[it] }
            )
        }

        // Voicing approximation for 3+ notes
        if (uniquePitchClasses.size >= 3) {
            val rootName = NOTE_NAMES[lowestPitchClass]
            val intervals = uniquePitchClasses.map { (it - lowestPitchClass + 12) % 12 }.sorted()

            val hasMaj3 = intervals.contains(4)
            val hasMin3 = intervals.contains(3)
            val hasPerf5 = intervals.contains(7)
            val hasDom7 = intervals.contains(10)
            val hasMaj7 = intervals.contains(11)
            val has9th = intervals.contains(2)
            val has11th = intervals.contains(5)
            val has13th = intervals.contains(9)

            val inferredSuffix = when {
                hasMaj3 && hasDom7 && has13th -> "13"
                hasMaj3 && hasMaj7 && has13th -> "maj13"
                hasMin3 && hasDom7 && has11th -> "m11"
                hasMaj3 && hasDom7 && has9th -> "9"
                hasMaj3 && hasMaj7 && has9th -> "maj9"
                hasMin3 && hasDom7 && has9th -> "m9"
                hasMaj3 && hasPerf5 && hasMaj7 -> "maj7"
                hasMaj3 && hasPerf5 && hasDom7 -> "7"
                hasMin3 && hasPerf5 && hasDom7 -> "m7"
                hasMaj3 && hasPerf5 -> ""
                hasMin3 && hasPerf5 -> "m"
                else -> ""
            }

            if (inferredSuffix.isNotEmpty()) {
                val notesFormatted = uniquePitchClasses.map { NOTE_NAMES[it] }.joinToString(" · ")
                return DetectedChord(
                    primaryName = "$rootName$inferredSuffix",
                    equivalentName = when (inferredSuffix) {
                        "maj7" -> "${rootName}Δ7"
                        "m7" -> "$rootName-7"
                        "7" -> "$rootName dom7"
                        else -> ""
                    },
                    variantName = "",
                    formula = notesFormatted,
                    notesList = uniquePitchClasses.map { NOTE_NAMES[it] }
                )
            }
        }

        // Clean fallback: root note without fake "Harmonie" or "Cluster"
        val rootName = NOTE_NAMES[lowestPitchClass]
        val clusterStr = uniquePitchClasses.map { NOTE_NAMES[it] }.joinToString(" · ")
        return DetectedChord(
            primaryName = rootName,
            equivalentName = "",
            variantName = "",
            formula = clusterStr,
            notesList = uniquePitchClasses.map { NOTE_NAMES[it] }
        )
    }
}

##############################################################################
# 3. FONCTIONS METIER (signatures REELLES + corps)
##############################################################################

Correspondance avec la liste demandee (a droite = nom reel a cabler) :

  addSnapshot(name: String)              -> addSnapshotSlot(name: String)        (l.4346)
  deleteSnapshot(id: String)             -> removeSnapshotSlot(slotKey: String)  (l.4401)
  updateSnapshot(id,newName,recallMode)  -> renameSnapshotSlot(slotName,newName) (l.5033)
                                            (aucun `recallMode` dans le modele -> cf. 7)
  loadSnapshot(id: String)               -> onSnapshotSlotClick(slotName)        (l.5051)
                                            ou recallSnapshotDirect(slotName)   (l.6399)
  setMasterVolume(value: Float)          -> setTrackVolume(trackId = 0, volume)  (l.2155)
  setBpm(value: Int)                     -> setGlobalBpm(newBpm: Int)            (l.1902)
                                            (relatif : updateBpm(delta))        (l.1898)
  setSustain(value: Float)               -> toggleSustain()                     (l.2105)
                                            (booleen, pas de Float -> cf. 7)
  sendPanic() / All Notes Off            -> triggerPanic()                      (l.2119)
                                            et panicAllNotesOff()               (l.6183)

Autres fonctions utiles au cablage :
  setTrackVolume(trackId, volume)  (l.2155)   setTrackPan(trackId, pan)        (l.2187)
  toggleTrackPower(trackId)        (l.2203)   onTrackMuteClick(trackId)        (l.2236)
  onTrackSoloClick(trackId)        (l.2255)   updateTranspose(delta)           (l.1878)
  updateOctave(delta)              (l.1889)   toggleSnapshotArm()              (l.5029)
  selectPrevSnapshot()             (l.6206)   selectNextSnapshot()             (l.6215)
  openPopup(popup)                 (l.2416)   closePopup()                     (l.2432)
  navigateToPage(page)             (l.5684)   navigateToProjectsHome()         (l.4523)
  openSettingsDrawer()             (l.5328)   saveCurrentProjectDirect()       (l.4209)
  toggleLoopsPanel()               (l.1315)   refreshStorageFiles()            (l.1050)
================================================================================


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.addSnapshotSlot
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun addSnapshotSlot(name: String) {
        val cleanName = name.trim().ifEmpty { "Snapshot ${_uiState.value.configuredSnapshotSlots.size}" }
        _uiState.update { current ->
            if (current.configuredSnapshotSlots.size < 5) {
                val newSlotKey = "slot_${System.currentTimeMillis()}"
                val updatedSlots = current.configuredSnapshotSlots + newSlotKey
                val updatedNames = current.snapshotCustomNames + (newSlotKey to cleanName)
                val currentSnapshot = SubSceneSnapshot(
                    slotName = cleanName,
                    tracks = current.tracks.map { t ->
                        TrackSnapshot(
                            id = t.id,
                            volume = t.volume,
                            pan = t.pan,
                            isMuted = t.isMuted,
                            isSolo = t.isSolo,
                            isEnabled = t.isEnabled,
                            soundfontName = t.soundfontName,
                            patchName = t.patchName,
                            bank = t.bank,
                            program = t.program,
                            reverbSend = t.reverbMix,
                            splitNoteMin = t.splitNoteMin,
                            splitNoteMax = t.splitNoteMax,
                            velocityCurve = t.velocityCurve
                        )
                    },
                    globalTranspose = current.transpose,
                    globalOctaveShift = current.octave,
                    masterVolume = current.masterTrack.volume,
                    visibleTracksCount = current.visibleTracksCount
                )
                val updatedSnapshots = current.snapshots + (newSlotKey to currentSnapshot)
                val updatedPatches = current.patches.map { p ->
                    if (p.id == current.activePatchId) {
                        p.copy(
                            configuredSnapshotSlots = updatedSlots,
                            snapshotCustomNames = updatedNames,
                            snapshots = updatedSnapshots,
                            activeSnapshotSlot = newSlotKey
                        )
                    } else p
                }
                current.copy(
                    configuredSnapshotSlots = updatedSlots,
                    snapshotCustomNames = updatedNames,
                    snapshots = updatedSnapshots,
                    activeSnapshotSlot = newSlotKey,
                    patches = updatedPatches
                )
            } else current
        }
        persistCurrentStateDebounced()
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.removeSnapshotSlot
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun removeSnapshotSlot(slotKey: String) {
        if (slotKey == "slot_default") return
        _uiState.update { current ->
            val updatedSlots = current.configuredSnapshotSlots.filter { it != slotKey }
            val updatedNames = current.snapshotCustomNames - slotKey
            val updatedSnapshots = current.snapshots - slotKey
            val newActive = if (current.activeSnapshotSlot == slotKey) "slot_default" else current.activeSnapshotSlot
            val updatedPatches = current.patches.map { p ->
                if (p.id == current.activePatchId) {
                    p.copy(
                        configuredSnapshotSlots = updatedSlots,
                        snapshotCustomNames = updatedNames,
                        snapshots = updatedSnapshots,
                        activeSnapshotSlot = newActive
                    )
                } else p
            }
            current.copy(
                configuredSnapshotSlots = updatedSlots,
                snapshotCustomNames = updatedNames,
                snapshots = updatedSnapshots,
                activeSnapshotSlot = newActive,
                patches = updatedPatches
            )
        }
        persistCurrentStateDebounced()
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.renameSnapshotSlot
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun renameSnapshotSlot(slotName: String, newName: String) {
        val trimmed = newName.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return
        _uiState.update { current ->
            val updated = current.snapshotCustomNames + (slotName to trimmed)
            val updatedPatches = current.patches.map { p ->
                if (p.id == current.activePatchId) {
                    p.copy(snapshotCustomNames = updated)
                } else p
            }
            current.copy(
                snapshotCustomNames = updated,
                patches = updatedPatches
            )
        }
        persistCurrentStateDebounced()
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.onSnapshotSlotClick
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun onSnapshotSlotClick(slotName: String) {
        val currentState = _uiState.value
        if (currentState.isSnapshotArmMode) {
            val trackSnapshots = currentState.tracks.map { t ->
                TrackSnapshot(
                    id = t.id,
                    volume = t.volume,
                    pan = t.pan,
                    isMuted = t.isMuted,
                    isSolo = t.isSolo,
                    isEnabled = t.isEnabled,
                    soundfontName = t.soundfontName,
                    patchName = t.patchName,
                    bank = t.bank,
                    program = t.program,
                    reverbSend = t.reverbMix,
                    splitNoteMin = t.splitNoteMin,
                    splitNoteMax = t.splitNoteMax,
                    velocityCurve = t.velocityCurve
                )
            }
            val newSubScene = SubSceneSnapshot(
                slotName = slotName,
                tracks = trackSnapshots,
                globalTranspose = currentState.transpose,
                globalOctaveShift = currentState.octave,
                masterVolume = currentState.tracks.find { it.isMaster }?.volume ?: 0.85f,
                visibleTracksCount = currentState.visibleTracksCount,
                fxParameters = currentState.fxParameters[0]
            )
            val updatedSnapshots = currentState.snapshots + (slotName to newSubScene)
            val updatedPatches = currentState.patches.map { p ->
                if (p.id == currentState.activePatchId) {
                    p.copy(
                        snapshots = updatedSnapshots,
                        activeSnapshotSlot = slotName
                    )
                } else p
            }
            _uiState.update {
                it.copy(
                    isSnapshotArmMode = false,
                    activeSnapshotSlot = slotName,
                    snapshots = updatedSnapshots,
                    patches = updatedPatches,
                    snapshotTransitionProgress = 1.0f
                )
            }
            persistCurrentState()
        } else {
            snapshotTransitionJob?.cancel()
            val startTracks = currentState.tracks

            if (currentState.activeSnapshotSlot == slotName) {
                // 2nd tap -> Return to Custom Libre state smoothly
                val freeTracks = currentState.customLibreTracks
                if (freeTracks != null) {
                    _uiState.update { state ->
                        state.copy(
                            activeSnapshotSlot = null,
                            snapshotTransitionProgress = 0.0f
                        )
                    }
                    persistCurrentStateDebounced()
                    snapshotTransitionJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                        val totalDurationMs = 1200L
                        val steps = 48
                        val stepDelay = totalDurationMs / steps
                        for (step in 1..steps) {
                            val linearProgress = step.toFloat() / steps
                            val eased = (1f - kotlin.math.cos(linearProgress * kotlin.math.PI.toFloat())) / 2f

                            val interpolated = startTracks.mapIndexed { idx, startTr ->
                                val targetTr = freeTracks.getOrNull(idx) ?: startTr
                                startTr.copy(
                                    volume = startTr.volume + (targetTr.volume - startTr.volume) * eased,
                                    pan = startTr.pan + (targetTr.pan - startTr.pan) * eased,
                                    isMuted = if (eased >= 0.5f) targetTr.isMuted else startTr.isMuted,
                                    isSolo = if (eased >= 0.5f) targetTr.isSolo else startTr.isSolo,
                                    isEnabled = if (eased >= 0.5f) targetTr.isEnabled else startTr.isEnabled
                                )
                            }
                            _uiState.update {
                                it.copy(tracks = interpolated, snapshotTransitionProgress = linearProgress)
                            }
                            interpolated.forEachIndexed { idx, track ->
                                val effectiveVol = if (track.isMuted || !track.isEnabled) 0f else track.volume
                                NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackVolume(idx, effectiveVol)
                                NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackPan(idx, track.pan)
                            }
                            delay(stepDelay)
                        }
                        _uiState.update {
                            it.copy(tracks = freeTracks, snapshotTransitionProgress = 1.0f)
                        }
                        persistCurrentState()
                    }
                } else {
                    _uiState.update { it.copy(activeSnapshotSlot = null, snapshotTransitionProgress = 1.0f) }
                    persistCurrentState()
                }
            } else {
                // 1st tap (or switching slot)
                val targetSnapshot = currentState.snapshots[slotName] ?: if (slotName == "slot_default") {
                    val activePatch = currentState.patches.find { it.id == currentState.activePatchId }
                    activePatch?.snapshots?.get("slot_default") ?: SubSceneSnapshot(
                        slotName = "Default",
                        tracks = (activePatch?.tracks ?: currentState.tracks).map { t ->
                            TrackSnapshot(
                                id = t.id,
                                volume = t.volume,
                                pan = t.pan,
                                isMuted = t.isMuted,
                                isSolo = t.isSolo,
                                isEnabled = t.isEnabled,
                                soundfontName = t.soundfontName,
                                patchName = t.patchName,
                                bank = t.bank,
                                program = t.program,
                                reverbSend = t.reverbMix,
                                splitNoteMin = t.splitNoteMin,
                                splitNoteMax = t.splitNoteMax,
                                velocityCurve = t.velocityCurve
                            )
                        },
                        globalTranspose = activePatch?.transpose ?: currentState.transpose,
                        globalOctaveShift = activePatch?.octave ?: currentState.octave,
                        masterVolume = activePatch?.masterVolume ?: currentState.masterTrack.volume,
                        visibleTracksCount = activePatch?.visibleTracksCount ?: currentState.visibleTracksCount
                    )
                } else null

                if (targetSnapshot != null) {
                    val savedCustomTracks = currentState.customLibreTracks ?: currentState.tracks
                    val targetTracks = currentState.tracks.map { tr ->
                        val snap = targetSnapshot.tracks.find { it.id == tr.id }
                        if (snap != null) {
                            val sfName = if (snap.soundfontName.isNotEmpty()) snap.soundfontName else tr.soundfontName
                            val patchName = if (snap.soundfontName.isNotEmpty()) snap.patchName else tr.patchName
                            tr.copy(
                                volume = snap.volume,
                                pan = snap.pan,
                                isMuted = snap.isMuted,
                                isSolo = snap.isSolo,
                                isEnabled = snap.isEnabled,
                                soundfontName = sfName,
                                patchName = patchName,
                                bank = snap.bank,
                                program = snap.program,
                                splitNoteMin = snap.splitNoteMin,
                                splitNoteMax = snap.splitNoteMax,
                                velocityCurve = snap.velocityCurve
                            )
                        } else {
                            // Track was added AFTER this snapshot was saved:
                            // Keep soundfont and settings, but deactivate/mute it to protect the snapshot mix
                            tr.copy(
                                isEnabled = false,
                                isMuted = true
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // Cut off notes immediately on tracks that are turning off or muting to avoid hanging voices
                    targetTracks.forEachIndexed { idx, track ->
                        if (!track.isEnabled || track.isMuted) {
                            audioEngine.setChannelEnabled(idx, false)
                            if (idx in 0..7) {
                                NativeAudioBridge.safeAllNotesOff(idx)
                            }
                        }
                        if (track.soundfontName.isNotEmpty()) {
                            val candidate = File(fileManager.soundfontsDir, track.soundfontName)
                            val currentSlot = currentState.audioSlots.getOrNull(idx)
                            if (currentSlot != null && currentSlot.soundFontId > 0 &&
                                (currentSlot.soundFontPath == candidate.absolutePath || currentSlot.soundFontPath?.endsWith(track.soundfontName) == true)
                            ) {
                                // Already loaded in memory! Just switch preset & bank directly without re-reading file or leaking RAM
                                audioEngine.setChannelProgram(idx, track.program, track.bank)
                                NativeAudioBridge.safeSelectProgram(
                                    engineIndex = NativeAudioBridge.ENGINE_FADER,
                                    channel = idx,
                                    soundFontId = currentSlot.soundFontId,
                                    bank = track.bank,
                                    preset = track.program
                                )
                                val effectiveVol = if (track.isMuted || !track.isEnabled) 0f else track.volume
                                NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackVolume(idx, effectiveVol)
                            } else if (candidate.exists()) {
                                loadSoundFontForSlot(idx, candidate.absolutePath, track.bank, track.program, track.patchName, saveAfterLoad = false)
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Update AudioEngine globals directly
                    audioEngine.globalOctaveShift = targetSnapshot.globalOctaveShift
                    audioEngine.globalTranspose = targetSnapshot.globalTranspose

                    // Apply mute and solo states immediately to state and audio engine
                    val initialInterpTracks = startTracks.mapIndexed { idx, startTr ->
                        val targetTr = targetTracks.getOrNull(idx) ?: startTr
                        startTr.copy(
                            isMuted = targetTr.isMuted,
                            isSolo = targetTr.isSolo,
                            isEnabled = targetTr.isEnabled
                        )
                    }

                    _uiState.update {
                        it.copy(
                            tracks = initialInterpTracks,
                            customLibreTracks = savedCustomTracks,
                            activeSnapshotSlot = slotName,
                            snapshotTransitionProgress = 0.0f,
                            transpose = targetSnapshot.globalTranspose,
                            octave = targetSnapshot.globalOctaveShift,
                            visibleTracksCount = maxOf(currentState.visibleTracksCount, if (targetSnapshot.visibleTracksCount in 1..8) targetSnapshot.visibleTracksCount else currentState.visibleTracksCount)
                        )
                    }
                    applyTrackVolumes(_uiState.value)
                    persistCurrentState()

                    snapshotTransitionJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                        val totalDurationMs = 600L
                        val steps = 24
                        val stepDelay = totalDurationMs / steps
                        for (step in 1..steps) {
                            val linearProgress = step.toFloat() / steps
                            // Cosine progressive interpolation for smooth volume glide
                            val eased = (1f - kotlin.math.cos(linearProgress * kotlin.math.PI.toFloat())) / 2f

                            val interpolated = startTracks.mapIndexed { idx, startTr ->
                                val targetTr = targetTracks.getOrNull(idx) ?: startTr
                                startTr.copy(
                                    volume = startTr.volume + (targetTr.volume - startTr.volume) * eased,
                                    pan = startTr.pan + (targetTr.pan - startTr.pan) * eased,
                                    isMuted = targetTr.isMuted,
                                    isSolo = targetTr.isSolo,
                                    isEnabled = targetTr.isEnabled
                                )
                            }
                            _uiState.update {
                                it.copy(tracks = interpolated, snapshotTransitionProgress = linearProgress)
                            }
                            interpolated.forEachIndexed { idx, track ->
                                val effectiveVol = if (track.isMuted || !track.isEnabled) 0f else track.volume
                                NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackVolume(idx, effectiveVol)
                                NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackPan(idx, track.pan)
                            }
                            delay(stepDelay)
                        }
                        _uiState.update {
                            it.copy(tracks = targetTracks, snapshotTransitionProgress = 1.0f)
                        }
                        applyTrackVolumes(_uiState.value)
                        persistCurrentStateDebounced()
                    }
                } else {
                    _uiState.update { it.copy(activeSnapshotSlot = slotName, snapshotTransitionProgress = 1.0f) }
                    persistCurrentStateDebounced()
                }
            }
        }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.recallSnapshotDirect
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun recallSnapshotDirect(slotName: String) {
        val currentState = _uiState.value
        snapshotTransitionJob?.cancel()

        val activePatch = currentState.patches.find { it.id == currentState.activePatchId }
        val targetSnapshot = currentState.snapshots[slotName]
            ?: activePatch?.snapshots?.get(slotName)
            ?: if (slotName == "slot_default") {
                activePatch?.snapshots?.get("slot_default") ?: SubSceneSnapshot(
                    slotName = "Default",
                    tracks = (activePatch?.tracks ?: currentState.tracks).map { t ->
                        TrackSnapshot(
                            id = t.id,
                            volume = t.volume,
                            pan = t.pan,
                            isMuted = t.isMuted,
                            isSolo = t.isSolo,
                            isEnabled = t.isEnabled,
                            soundfontName = t.soundfontName,
                            patchName = t.patchName,
                            bank = t.bank,
                            program = t.program,
                            reverbSend = t.reverbMix,
                            splitNoteMin = t.splitNoteMin,
                            splitNoteMax = t.splitNoteMax,
                            velocityCurve = t.velocityCurve
                        )
                    },
                    globalTranspose = activePatch?.transpose ?: currentState.transpose,
                    globalOctaveShift = activePatch?.octave ?: currentState.octave,
                    masterVolume = activePatch?.masterVolume ?: currentState.masterTrack.volume,
                    visibleTracksCount = activePatch?.visibleTracksCount ?: currentState.visibleTracksCount
                )
            } else null

        if (targetSnapshot != null) {
            val targetTracks = currentState.tracks.map { tr ->
                val snap = targetSnapshot.tracks.find { it.id == tr.id }
                if (snap != null) {
                    val sfName = if (snap.soundfontName.isNotEmpty()) snap.soundfontName else tr.soundfontName
                    val patchName = if (snap.soundfontName.isNotEmpty()) snap.patchName else tr.patchName
                    tr.copy(
                        volume = snap.volume,
                        pan = snap.pan,
                        isMuted = snap.isMuted,
                        isSolo = snap.isSolo,
                        isEnabled = snap.isEnabled,
                        soundfontName = sfName,
                        patchName = patchName,
                        bank = snap.bank,
                        program = snap.program,
                        splitNoteMin = snap.splitNoteMin,
                        splitNoteMax = snap.splitNoteMax,
                        velocityCurve = snap.velocityCurve
                    )
                } else {
                    tr.copy(isEnabled = false, isMuted = true)
                }
            }

            targetTracks.forEachIndexed { idx, track ->
                if (!track.isEnabled || track.isMuted) {
                    audioEngine.setChannelEnabled(idx, false)
                    if (idx in 0..7) {
                        NativeAudioBridge.safeAllNotesOff(idx)
                    }
                }
                if (track.soundfontName.isNotEmpty()) {
                    val candidate = File(fileManager.soundfontsDir, track.soundfontName)
                    val currentSlot = currentState.audioSlots.getOrNull(idx)
                    if (currentSlot != null && currentSlot.soundFontId > 0 &&
                        (currentSlot.soundFontPath == candidate.absolutePath || currentSlot.soundFontPath?.endsWith(track.soundfontName) == true)
                    ) {
                        audioEngine.setChannelProgram(idx, track.program, track.bank)
                        NativeAudioBridge.safeSelectProgram(
                            engineIndex = NativeAudioBridge.ENGINE_FADER,
                            channel = idx,
                            soundFontId = currentSlot.soundFontId,
                            bank = track.bank,
                            preset = track.program
                        )
                        val effectiveVol = if (track.isMuted || !track.isEnabled) 0f else track.volume
                        NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackVolume(idx, effectiveVol)
                    } else if (candidate.exists()) {
                        loadSoundFontForSlot(idx, candidate.absolutePath, track.bank, track.program, track.patchName, saveAfterLoad = false)
                    }
                }
            }

            audioEngine.globalOctaveShift = targetSnapshot.globalOctaveShift
            audioEngine.globalTranspose = targetSnapshot.globalTranspose

            targetTracks.forEachIndexed { idx, track ->
                val effectiveVol = if (track.isMuted || !track.isEnabled) 0f else track.volume
                NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackVolume(idx, effectiveVol)
                NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackPan(idx, track.pan)
            }

            _uiState.update { state ->
                state.copy(
                    activeSnapshotSlot = slotName,
                    tracks = targetTracks,
                    transpose = targetSnapshot.globalTranspose,
                    octave = targetSnapshot.globalOctaveShift,
                    visibleTracksCount = targetSnapshot.visibleTracksCount,
                    snapshotTransitionProgress = 1.0f
                )
            }
            persistCurrentStateDebounced()
        }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.setTrackVolume
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun setTrackVolume(trackId: Int, volume: Float) {
        val clampedVol = volume.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        if (trackId == 0) {
            audioEngine.masterVolume = clampedVol
            NativeAudioBridge.safeSetMasterVolume(clampedVol)
            _uiState.update { state ->
                state.copy(masterTrack = state.masterTrack.copy(volume = clampedVol))
            }
        } else if (trackId in 1..8) {
            val ch = trackId - 1
            val currentState = _uiState.value
            val targetTrack = currentState.tracks.getOrNull(ch)
            val anySolo = currentState.tracks.any { it.isSolo }
            val effectiveVol = when {
                targetTrack == null || !targetTrack.isEnabled -> 0f
                anySolo && !targetTrack.isSolo -> 0f
                targetTrack.isMuted -> 0f
                else -> clampedVol
            }
            audioEngine.setChannelVolume(ch, effectiveVol)
            NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackVolume(ch, effectiveVol)

            _uiState.update { state ->
                val updated = state.tracks.map { track ->
                    if (track.id == trackId) track.copy(volume = clampedVol) else track
                }
                state.copy(tracks = updated)
            }
        }
        persistCurrentStateDebounced()
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.setGlobalBpm
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun setGlobalBpm(newBpm: Int) {
        val bpm = newBpm.coerceIn(20, 300)
        _uiState.update { it.copy(bpm = bpm) }
        if (_uiState.value.isMetronomeOn) {
            audioEngine.startMetronome(bpm, _uiState.value.metronomeSignature, _uiState.value.metronomeVolume)
        }
        if (_uiState.value.isLoopPlaying) {
            audioEngine.setLoopBpm(bpm)
        }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.toggleSustain
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    fun toggleSustain() {
        val nextSustain = !_uiState.value.isSustainActive
        audioEngine.setSustainPedal(nextSustain)
        _uiState.update { state ->
            state.copy(
                isSustainActive = nextSustain
            )
        }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.triggerPanic
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun triggerPanic() {
        audioEngine.setSustainPedal(false)
        audioEngine.allNotesOff()
        audioEngine.stopMetronome()
        audioEngine.stopLoopPlayer()
        audioEngine.stopMidiPlayer()
        _uiState.update { state ->
            state.copy(
                pressedKeys = emptySet(),
                activeTonicNotes = emptySet(),
                isSustainActive = false,
                isLoopPlaying = false,
                isMidiPlaying = false,
                isMetronomeOn = false
            )
        }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.panicAllNotesOff
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun panicAllNotesOff() {
        audioEngine.allNotesOff()
        _uiState.update { it.copy(pressedKeys = emptySet(), isMidiPedalPressed = false) }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.setTrackPan
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun setTrackPan(trackId: Int, pan: Float) {
        val clampedPan = pan.coerceIn(-1f, 1f)
        if (trackId in 1..8) {
            val ch = trackId - 1
            audioEngine.setChannelPan(ch, clampedPan)
            NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackPan(ch, clampedPan)
        }
        _uiState.update { state ->
            val updated = state.tracks.map { track ->
                if (track.id == trackId) track.copy(pan = clampedPan) else track
            }
            state.copy(tracks = updated)
        }
        persistCurrentStateDebounced()
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.toggleTrackPower
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun toggleTrackPower(trackId: Int) {
        _uiState.update { state ->
            val updated = state.tracks.map { track ->
                if (track.id == trackId) {
                    val nextState = !track.isEnabled
                    if (trackId in 1..8) {
                        val ch = trackId - 1
                        audioEngine.setChannelEnabled(ch, nextState)
                        if (!nextState) {
                            NativeAudioBridge.safeAllNotesOff(ch)
                        } else {
                            val slot = state.audioSlots.getOrNull(ch)
                            if (slot != null && slot.soundFontId > 0) {
                                NativeAudioBridge.safeSelectProgram(
                                    engineIndex = NativeAudioBridge.ENGINE_FADER,
                                    channel = ch,
                                    soundFontId = slot.soundFontId,
                                    bank = slot.bank,
                                    preset = slot.preset
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    track.copy(isEnabled = nextState)
                } else track
            }
            val newState = state.copy(tracks = updated)
            applyTrackVolumes(newState)
            newState
        }
        persistCurrentStateDebounced()
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.onTrackMuteClick
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun onTrackMuteClick(trackId: Int) {
        _uiState.update { state ->
            val updated = state.tracks.map { t ->
                if (t.id == trackId) {
                    t.copy(isMuted = !t.isMuted)
                } else t
            }
            val newState = state.copy(tracks = updated)
            applyTrackVolumes(newState)
            
            val isNowMuted = newState.tracks.find { it.id == trackId }?.let { it.isMuted || (newState.tracks.any { t -> t.isSolo } && !it.isSolo) } == true
            if (isNowMuted && trackId in 1..8) {
                 NativeAudioBridge.safeAllNotesOff(trackId - 1)
            }
            
            newState
        }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.onTrackSoloClick
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun onTrackSoloClick(trackId: Int) {
        _uiState.update { state ->
            val updated = state.tracks.map { t ->
                if (t.id == trackId) {
                    t.copy(isSolo = !t.isSolo)
                } else t
            }
            val newState = state.copy(tracks = updated)
            applyTrackVolumes(newState)
            
            val isNowMuted = newState.tracks.find { it.id == trackId }?.let { it.isMuted || (newState.tracks.any { t -> t.isSolo } && !it.isSolo) } == true
            if (isNowMuted && trackId in 1..8) {
                 NativeAudioBridge.safeAllNotesOff(trackId - 1)
            }
            
            newState
        }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.updateTranspose
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    fun updateTranspose(delta: Int) {
        val newTranspose = (_uiState.value.transpose + delta).coerceIn(-12, 12)
        _uiState.update { it.copy(transpose = newTranspose) }
        audioEngine.globalTranspose = newTranspose
        // Update transpose on all 8 MIDI channels
        for (ch in 0..7) {
            NativeAudioBridge.safeSetTrackTranspose(ch, newTranspose)
        }
        persistCurrentStateDebounced()
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.updateOctave
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun updateOctave(delta: Int) {
        val newOctave = (_uiState.value.octave + delta).coerceIn(-3, 3)
        if (newOctave != _uiState.value.octave) {
            audioEngine.globalOctaveShift = newOctave
            _uiState.update { it.copy(octave = newOctave) }
            persistCurrentStateDebounced()
        }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.toggleSnapshotArm
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    fun toggleSnapshotArm() {
        _uiState.update { it.copy(isSnapshotArmMode = !it.isSnapshotArmMode) }
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.selectPrevSnapshot
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun selectPrevSnapshot() {
        val state = _uiState.value
        val slots = state.configuredSnapshotSlots
        if (slots.isEmpty()) return
        val currentIdx = slots.indexOf(state.activeSnapshotSlot)
        val prevIdx = if (currentIdx <= 0) slots.size - 1 else currentIdx - 1
        onSnapshotSlotClick(slots[prevIdx])
    }

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
### MixerViewModel.selectNextSnapshot
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun selectNextSnapshot() {
        val state = _uiState.value
        val slots = state.configuredSnapshotSlots
        if (slots.isEmpty()) return
        val currentIdx = slots.indexOf(state.activeSnapshotSlot)
        val nextIdx = if (currentIdx < 0 || currentIdx >= slots.size - 1) 0 else currentIdx + 1
        onSnapshotSlotClick(slots[nextIdx])
    }

##############################################################################
# 4. ECRAN PRINCIPAL (PARENT)
##############################################################################

MixerScreen(viewModel, modifier, onRequestManageStoragePermission) est l'ecran
racine ; MainActivity le monte sous SoundfontLiveMixerTheme. Les deux fichiers
sont fournis en entier ci-dessous.

                            // In Unlock mode: Always maintains default width (120.dp) with horizontal scroll.

                            val defaultTrackWidth = 120.dp

                            val totalNeededWidth = defaultTrackWidth * activeItemCount
                            val isOverflowingInLock = uiState.isFaderAutoFit && (totalNeededWidth > availableSpace)
                            val autoFitWidth = if (activeItemCount > 0) (availableSpace / activeItemCount) else defaultTrackWidth
                            val finalTrackWidth = if (isOverflowingInLock) autoFitWidth else defaultTrackWidth
